Method: 采用被试内实验设计，在自然复杂度逐渐增加的游戏环境中进行。收集早期眼动和心电信号，用于预测后期任务表现。建立眼动-心电融合模型和仅眼动模型进行预测分析，同时比较高低表现者在生理指标和自我报告体验上的差异。

Result: 眼动-心电融合模型达到0.86的平衡准确率，仅眼动模型也显示出相当的预测能力。高表现者表现出更有针对性的注视模式、适应性视觉采样，以及在需求增加时维持更稳定的心电激活，同时报告更积极的情感体验。

Conclusion: 研究证明了利用早期生理信号进行跨会话表现预测的可行性，为表现差异提供了可解释的生理机制见解，有助于未来开发主动干预系统。

Abstract: User performance is crucial in interactive systems, capturing how effectively users engage with task execution. Prospectively predicting performance enables the timely identification of users struggling with task demands. While ocular and cardiac signals are widely used to characterise performance-relevant visual behaviour and physiological activation, their potential for early prediction and for revealing the physiological mechanisms underlying performance differences remains underexplored. We conducted a within-subject experiment in a game environment with naturally unfolding complexity, using early ocular and cardiac signals to predict later performance and to examine physiological and self-reported group differences. Results show that the ocular-cardiac fusion model achieves a balanced accuracy of 0.86, and the ocular-only model shows comparable predictive power. High performers exhibited targeted gaze and adjusted visual sampling, and sustained more stable cardiac activation as demands intensified, with a more positive affective experience. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of cross-session prediction from early physiology, providing interpretable insights into performance variation and facilitating future proactive intervention.

</details>


### [156] [Seasoning Generative Models for a Generalization Aftertaste](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18817)
*Hisham Husain,Valentin De Bortoli,Richard Nock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于判别器引导的生成模型精炼方法，通过扩展f-散度的强对偶性，为任何生成模型提供理论保证的精炼框架，并证明精炼后的模型在泛化性能上优于原始模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管判别器在训练生成模型（如GANs）中已证明有效，且最近扩散模型也显示出判别器引导的优势，但现有方法缺乏理论保证。作者旨在开发一个具有理论基础的判别器引导框架，能够精炼任何生成模型，并提供泛化性能的严格分析。

Method: 扩展了f-散度的强对偶性结果，提出了一个判别器引导的精炼方法。该方法可以应用于任何生成模型，通过利用判别器集合来改进模型。分析基于判别器集合的Rademacher复杂度来量化泛化性能的提升。

Result: 理论证明精炼后的生成模型在泛化性能上优于未精炼的原始模型，泛化差距的改进取决于用于精炼的判别器集合的Rademacher复杂度。该框架包含了Kim等人（2022）的基于分数的扩散方法，并为其提供了理论验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为现有判别器引导方法提供了理论验证，提出了新算法的方向，并增进了对生成模型泛化性能的理解。提出的精炼框架具有普适性，能够提升任何生成模型的泛化能力。

Abstract: The use of discriminators to train or fine-tune generative models has proven to be a rather successful framework. A notable example is Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) that minimize a loss incurred by training discriminators along with other paradigms that boost generative models via discriminators that satisfy weak learner constraints. More recently, even diffusion models have shown advantages with some kind of discriminator guidance. In this work, we extend a strong-duality result related to $f$-divergences which gives rise to a discriminator-guided recipe that allows us to \textit{refine} any generative model. We then show that the refined generative models provably improve generalization, compared to its non-refined counterpart. In particular, our analysis reveals that the gap in generalization is improved based on the Rademacher complexity of the discriminator set used for refinement. Our recipe subsumes a recently introduced score-based diffusion approach (Kim et al., 2022) that has shown great empirical success, however allows us to shed light on the generalization guarantees of this method by virtue of our analysis. Thus, our work provides a theoretical validation for existing work, suggests avenues for new algorithms, and contributes to our understanding of generalization in generative models at large.

</details>


### [157] [A Model Ensemble-Based Post-Processing Framework for Fairness-Aware Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18838)
*Zhouting Zhao,Tin Lok James Ng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模型集成的后处理框架，在保持预测性能的同时提升机器学习模型的公平性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器学习中，预测性能与公平性之间的平衡是一个基本挑战，需要开发既能保持预测准确性又能提升公平性的通用方法

Method: 提出一个基于模型集成的后处理框架，该框架不依赖于特定模型内部结构，可广泛应用于各种学习任务、模型架构和公平性定义

Result: 通过分类、回归和生存分析等广泛实验证明，该框架能有效提升公平性，同时保持或仅轻微影响预测准确性

Conclusion: 提出的后处理框架为机器学习中的公平性-性能权衡问题提供了一个通用且有效的解决方案

Abstract: Striking an optimal balance between predictive performance and fairness continues to be a fundamental challenge in machine learning. In this work, we propose a post-processing framework that facilitates fairness-aware prediction by leveraging model ensembling. Designed to operate independently of any specific model internals, our approach is widely applicable across various learning tasks, model architectures, and fairness definitions. Through extensive experiments spanning classification, regression, and survival analysis, we demonstrate that the framework effectively enhances fairness while maintaining, or only minimally affecting, predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [158] [DriftGuard: Mitigating Asynchronous Data Drift in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18872)
*Yizhou Han,Di Wu,Blesson Varghese*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DriftGuard是一个联邦持续学习框架，采用混合专家架构分离共享参数和本地参数，通过全局重训练和分组重训练策略高效适应异步数据漂移，在保持高精度的同时将重训练成本降低达83%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界联邦学习中，参与训练设备的数据分布会随时间演变，导致不同设备在不同时间向不同分布漂移的异步数据漂移问题。频繁重训练在资源受限设备上计算成本高，而不频繁重训练则会导致漂移设备性能下降，需要一种高效适应异步数据漂移的解决方案。

Method: 提出DriftGuard联邦持续学习框架，采用混合专家（MoE）架构，分离共享参数（捕获全局可迁移知识）和本地参数（适应组特定分布）。设计了两种互补的重训练策略：1）全局重训练：当识别到系统范围漂移时更新共享参数；2）分组重训练：通过MoE门控模式识别设备集群，选择性更新本地参数，无需共享原始数据。

Result: 在多个数据集和模型上的实验表明，DriftGuard匹配或超过最先进方法的精度，同时将总重训练成本降低达83%。实现了最高的单位重训练成本精度，比最强基线提高达2.3倍。

Conclusion: DriftGuard通过混合专家架构和双重重训练策略，有效解决了联邦学习中的异步数据漂移问题，在保持模型性能的同时显著降低了计算成本，为资源受限环境下的联邦持续学习提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: In real-world Federated Learning (FL) deployments, data distributions on devices that participate in training evolve over time. This leads to asynchronous data drift, where different devices shift at different times and toward different distributions. Mitigating such drift is challenging: frequent retraining incurs high computational cost on resource-constrained devices, while infrequent retraining degrades performance on drifting devices. We propose DriftGuard, a federated continual learning framework that efficiently adapts to asynchronous data drift. DriftGuard adopts a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inspired architecture that separates shared parameters, which capture globally transferable knowledge, from local parameters that adapt to group-specific distributions. This design enables two complementary retraining strategies: (i) global retraining, which updates the shared parameters when system-wide drift is identified, and (ii) group retraining, which selectively updates local parameters for clusters of devices identified via MoE gating patterns, without sharing raw data. Experiments across multiple datasets and models show that DriftGuard matches or exceeds state-of-the-art accuracy while reducing total retraining cost by up to 83%. As a result, it achieves the highest accuracy per unit retraining cost, improving over the strongest baseline by up to 2.3x. DriftGuard is available for download from https://github.com/blessonvar/DriftGuard.

</details>


### [159] [Context Bootstrapped Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18953)
*Saaket Agashe,Jayanth Srinivasa,Gaowen Liu,Ramana Kompella,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CBRL通过在训练提示中随机添加少量示例演示来增强RLVR，使用从高到零的课程概率，以引导早期探索并最终实现独立推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR存在探索效率低下的问题，模型难以生成成功的rollout，导致学习信号微弱。对于需要获取新颖推理模式或领域特定知识的任务，这一问题尤为严重

Method: 提出上下文引导强化学习（CBRL），通过在训练提示中随机前置少量示例演示来增强RLVR训练。注入概率遵循从高到零的课程安排：初始阶段高概率注入以引导早期探索，随后逐渐衰减至零，迫使模型最终无需辅助即可成功

Result: 在两个模型系列和五个Reasoning Gym任务上验证CBRL，结果显示CBRL持续提高成功率，提供更好的探索效率，且具有算法无关性。进一步在Q（一种与主流语言规范显著不同的领域特定编程语言）上展示了CBRL的实际应用性

Conclusion: CBRL通过引导式探索有效解决了RLVR的探索效率问题，使模型能够从演示中内化推理模式而非依赖测试时的辅助，在多种任务和模型上表现出普遍适用性

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) suffers from exploration inefficiency, where models struggle to generate successful rollouts, resulting in minimal learning signal. This challenge is particularly severe for tasks that require the acquisition of novel reasoning patterns or domain-specific knowledge. To address this, we propose Context Bootstrapped Reinforcement Learning (CBRL), which augments RLVR training by stochastically prepending few-shot demonstrations to training prompts. The injection probability follows a curriculum that starts high to bootstrap early exploration, then anneals to zero so the model must ultimately succeed without assistance. This forces the policy to internalize reasoning patterns from the demonstrations rather than relying on them at test time. We validate CBRL across two model families and five Reasoning Gym tasks. Our results demonstrate that CBRL consistently improves success rate, provides better exploration efficiency, and is algorithm-agnostic. We further demonstrate CBRL's practical applicability on Q, a domain-specific programming language that diverges significantly from mainstream language conventions.

</details>


### [160] [Online Learning and Equilibrium Computation with Ranking Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19221)
*Mingyang Liu,Yongshan Chen,Zhiyuan Fan,Gabriele Farina,Asuman Ozdaglar,Kaiqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了一种仅通过行动排序反馈（而非数值效用反馈）的在线学习模型，分析了瞬时效用排序和时间平均效用排序在完全信息和老虎机反馈下的可学习性，并提出了在效用序列具有次线性总变差假设下的次线性遗憾算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线学习算法依赖数值效用反馈，但在人机交互应用和隐私保护场景中，数值反馈可能不可用或受限。因此需要研究仅通过排序反馈进行学习的方法。

Method: 研究了两种排序机制：基于瞬时效用的排序和基于时间平均效用的排序，分别在完全信息和老虎机反馈设置下分析。提出了在效用序列具有次线性总变差假设下的新算法，并分析了Plackett-Luce排序模型下的学习界限。

Result: 证明在一般情况下，瞬时效用排序反馈无法实现次线性遗憾；在Plackett-Luce模型温度足够小时，时间平均效用排序反馈也无法实现次线性遗憾。但在效用序列具有次线性总变差假设下，提出的算法能够实现次线性遗憾。对于完全信息时间平均效用排序反馈，这一额外假设可以移除。

Conclusion: 仅通过排序反馈进行在线学习在某些条件下是可行的，提出的算法在正常形式博弈中能够产生近似粗相关均衡，并在在线大语言模型路由任务中展示了有效性。这为隐私保护和人机交互场景中的在线学习提供了新方法。

Abstract: Online learning in arbitrary, and possibly adversarial, environments has been extensively studied in sequential decision-making, and it is closely connected to equilibrium computation in game theory. Most existing online learning algorithms rely on \emph{numeric} utility feedback from the environment, which may be unavailable in human-in-the-loop applications and/or may be restricted by privacy concerns. In this paper, we study an online learning model in which the learner only observes a \emph{ranking} over a set of proposed actions at each timestep. We consider two ranking mechanisms: rankings induced by the \emph{instantaneous} utility at the current timestep, and rankings induced by the \emph{time-average} utility up to the current timestep, under both \emph{full-information} and \emph{bandit} feedback settings. Using the standard external-regret metric, we show that sublinear regret is impossible with instantaneous-utility ranking feedback in general. Moreover, when the ranking model is relatively deterministic, \emph{i.e.}, under the Plackett-Luce model with a temperature that is sufficiently small, sublinear regret is also impossible with time-average utility ranking feedback. We then develop new algorithms that achieve sublinear regret under the additional assumption that the utility sequence has sublinear total variation. Notably, for full-information time-average utility ranking feedback, this additional assumption can be removed. As a consequence, when all players in a normal-form game follow our algorithms, repeated play yields an approximate coarse correlated equilibrium. We also demonstrate the effectiveness of our algorithms in an online large-language-model routing task.

</details>


### [161] [BVSIMC: Bayesian Variable Selection-Guided Inductive Matrix Completion for Improved and Interpretable Drug Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18957)
*Sijian Fan,Liyan Xiong,Dayuan Wang,Guoshuai Cai,Ray Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BVSIMC方法，通过贝叶斯变量选择从药物发现的侧信息中筛选特征，学习稀疏潜在嵌入，提高预测准确性和可解释性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现中，侧信息（如药物化学性质和疾病基因组信息）能显著提升预测性能，但这些特征通常相关性差异大、噪声多且高维，需要有效筛选机制

Method: 提出贝叶斯变量选择引导的归纳矩阵补全（BVSIMC），通过贝叶斯框架实现侧信息的变量选择，学习稀疏潜在嵌入，在保持预测性能的同时提高模型可解释性

Result: 在合成数据和两个真实药物发现应用（结核分枝杆菌耐药性预测和药物重定位关联预测）中，BVSIMC均优于其他最先进方法，并能识别最具临床意义的侧特征

Conclusion: BVSIMC通过贝叶斯变量选择有效处理药物发现中的高维噪声侧信息，在提升预测性能的同时增强模型可解释性，为药物发现提供了有价值的分析工具

Abstract: Recent advances in drug discovery have demonstrated that incorporating side information (e.g., chemical properties about drugs and genomic information about diseases) often greatly improves prediction performance. However, these side features can vary widely in relevance and are often noisy and high-dimensional. We propose Bayesian Variable Selection-Guided Inductive Matrix Completion (BVSIMC), a new Bayesian model that enables variable selection from side features in drug discovery. By learning sparse latent embeddings, BVSIMC improves both predictive accuracy and interpretability. We validate our method through simulation studies and two drug discovery applications: 1) prediction of drug resistance in Mycobacterium tuberculosis, and 2) prediction of new drug-disease associations in computational drug repositioning. On both synthetic and real data, BVSIMC outperforms several other state-of-the-art methods in terms of prediction. In our two real examples, BVSIMC further reveals the most clinically meaningful side features.

</details>


### [162] [Maximum-Entropy Exploration with Future State-Action Visitation Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18965)
*Adrien Bolland,Gaspard Lambrechts,Damien Ernst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于状态-动作特征折扣分布熵的内在奖励方法，通过最大化轨迹中特征分布的熵来改进探索


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大熵强化学习通过最大化某些分布的熵来鼓励智能体探索状态和动作。本文研究基于未来时间步访问的状态-动作特征折扣分布熵的内在奖励方法，旨在改进特征在单个轨迹内的访问分布

Method: 提出内在奖励与未来时间步访问的状态-动作特征折扣分布的熵成比例。证明该内在奖励的期望和是初始状态开始轨迹中状态-动作特征折扣分布熵的下界，且该分布是收缩算子的不动点，可进行离策略估计

Result: 新目标导致单个轨迹内特征访问的改进，但以略微减少不同轨迹期望上的特征访问为代价。在纯探索智能体学习中提高了收敛速度，在大多数基准测试中控制性能保持相似

Conclusion: 基于状态-动作特征折扣分布熵的内在奖励方法能有效改进探索策略，特别是在单个轨迹内的特征访问分布方面，同时保持了控制性能

Abstract: Maximum entropy reinforcement learning motivates agents to explore states and actions to maximize the entropy of some distribution, typically by providing additional intrinsic rewards proportional to that entropy function. In this paper, we study intrinsic rewards proportional to the entropy of the discounted distribution of state-action features visited during future time steps. This approach is motivated by two results. First, we show that the expected sum of these intrinsic rewards is a lower bound on the entropy of the discounted distribution of state-action features visited in trajectories starting from the initial states, which we relate to an alternative maximum entropy objective. Second, we show that the distribution used in the intrinsic reward definition is the fixed point of a contraction operator and can therefore be estimated off-policy. Experiments highlight that the new objective leads to improved visitation of features within individual trajectories, in exchange for slightly reduced visitation of features in expectation over different trajectories, as suggested by the lower bound. It also leads to improved convergence speed for learning exploration-only agents. Control performance remains similar across most methods on the considered benchmarks.

</details>


### [163] [Best-of-Both-Worlds Multi-Dueling Bandits: Unified Algorithms for Stochastic and Adversarial Preferences under Condorcet and Borda Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18972)
*S. Akash,Pratik Gajane,Jawar Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了首个适用于随机和对抗环境的"两全其美"多决斗老虎机算法，针对Condorcet和Borda两种目标函数，无需预先知道所处环境类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多决斗老虎机在排名和推荐系统中自然出现，但一个基本问题尚未解决：是否存在单一算法能在随机和对抗环境中都表现最优，而无需知道面临哪种环境？

Method: 针对Condorcet设置提出了MetaDueling黑盒归约，将多路胜者反馈转换为无偏的成对信号；针对Borda设置提出了AlgBorda算法，结合了随机和对抗环境下的优化策略。

Result: MetaDueling结合Versatile-DB实现了O(√KT)对抗伪遗憾和实例最优O(∑_{i≠a*} logT/Δ_i)随机伪遗憾；AlgBorda在随机环境中达到O(K²logKT + Klog²T + ∑_{i:Δ_iᴮ>0} KlogKT/(Δ_iᴮ)²)遗憾，在对抗环境中达到O(K√TlogKT + K¹/³T²/³(logK)¹/³)遗憾。

Conclusion: 首次证明了存在单一算法能在随机和对抗环境中都表现最优，无需预先知道环境类型，填补了多决斗老虎机领域的重要空白，并提供了匹配的下界证明。

Abstract: Multi-dueling bandits, where a learner selects $m \geq 2$ arms per round and observes only the winner, arise naturally in many applications including ranking and recommendation systems, yet a fundamental question has remained open: can a single algorithm perform optimally in both stochastic and adversarial environments, without knowing which regime it faces? We answer this affirmatively, providing the first best-of-both-worlds algorithms for multi-dueling bandits under both Condorcet and Borda objectives. For the Condorcet setting, we propose \texttt{MetaDueling}, a black-box reduction that converts any dueling bandit algorithm into a multi-dueling bandit algorithm by transforming multi-way winner feedback into an unbiased pairwise signal. Instantiating our reduction with \texttt{Versatile-DB} yields the first best-of-both-worlds algorithm for multi-dueling bandits: it achieves $O(\sqrt{KT})$ pseudo-regret against adversarial preferences and the instance-optimal $O\!\left(\sum_{i \neq a^\star} \frac{\log T}{Δ_i}\right)$ pseudo-regret under stochastic preferences, both simultaneously and without prior knowledge of the regime. For the Borda setting, we propose \AlgBorda, a stochastic-and-adversarial algorithm that achieves $O\left(K^2 \log KT + K \log^2 T + \sum_{i: Δ_i^{\mathrm{B}} > 0} \frac{K\log KT}{(Δ_i^{\mathrm{B}})^2}\right)$ regret in stochastic environments and $O\left(K \sqrt{T \log KT} + K^{1/3} T^{2/3} (\log K)^{1/3}\right)$ regret against adversaries, again without prior knowledge of the regime. We complement our upper bounds with matching lower bounds for the Condorcet setting. For the Borda setting, our upper bounds are near-optimal with respect to the lower bounds (within a factor of $K$) and match the best-known results in the literature.

</details>


### [164] [Foundations of Schrödinger Bridges for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18992)
*Sophia Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指南系统性地阐述了薛定谔桥问题的数学基础及其在现代生成建模中的核心作用，提供了从第一原理构建薛定谔桥的完整工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成建模框架（扩散模型、基于分数的模型、流匹配等）的核心任务是通过概率空间中的随机路径将简单先验分布转换为复杂目标分布。薛定谔桥为这些方法提供了统一的理论基础，但需要系统性的数学阐述和实用工具包。

Method: 基于最优传输、随机控制和路径空间优化的数学基础，开发薛定谔桥问题的动态公式化方法。从第一原理构建薛定谔桥，建立与现代生成建模的直接联系，并推导出广义和任务特定的计算方法。

Result: 构建了全面的薛定谔桥构造工具包，展示了这些构造如何产生广义和任务特定的计算方法，为现代生成建模提供了统一的理论框架和实用实现方案。

Conclusion: 薛定谔桥问题为现代生成建模方法提供了统一的理论基础，其动态公式化与生成建模直接相关。通过系统性的数学阐述和工具包构建，该指南为理解和实现基于薛定谔桥的生成模型提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: At the core of modern generative modeling frameworks, including diffusion models, score-based models, and flow matching, is the task of transforming a simple prior distribution into a complex target distribution through stochastic paths in probability space. Schrödinger bridges provide a unifying principle underlying these approaches, framing the problem as determining an optimal stochastic bridge between marginal distribution constraints with minimal-entropy deviations from a pre-defined reference process. This guide develops the mathematical foundations of the Schrödinger bridge problem, drawing on optimal transport, stochastic control, and path-space optimization, and focuses on its dynamic formulation with direct connections to modern generative modeling. We build a comprehensive toolkit for constructing Schrödinger bridges from first principles, and show how these constructions give rise to generalized and task-specific computational methods.

</details>


### [165] [When Differential Privacy Meets Wireless Federated Learning: An Improved Analysis for Privacy and Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19040)
*Chen Yaoling,Liang Hao,Tu Xiaotong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对差分隐私无线联邦学习进行了全面分析，解决了现有工作中隐私损失表征不精确、收敛分析依赖严格凸性假设或忽略梯度裁剪影响的问题，为非凸损失目标建立了隐私与收敛的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私无线联邦学习（DPWFL）是保护敏感用户数据的有前景框架，但现有工作存在两个主要问题：1）如何精确表征隐私损失的基础性问题尚未解决；2）收敛分析要么依赖限制性的凸性假设，要么忽略了梯度裁剪的影响。

Method: 提出了DPWFL的全面隐私与收敛分析方法，适用于一般光滑非凸损失目标。分析明确纳入了设备选择和mini-batch采样，并考虑了梯度裁剪的影响。建立了收敛保证并推导出显式的隐私-效用权衡关系。

Result: 理论分析表明：1）隐私损失可以收敛到一个常数而非随迭代次数发散；2）在梯度裁剪下建立了收敛保证；3）推导出显式的隐私-效用权衡关系。数值结果验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 该研究克服了现有DPWFL分析的局限性，为非凸损失目标提供了全面的隐私与收敛理论框架，证明了隐私损失可以收敛到常数，并建立了包含梯度裁剪的收敛保证和隐私-效用权衡关系。

Abstract: Differentially private wireless federated learning (DPWFL) is a promising framework for protecting sensitive user data. However, foundational questions on how to precisely characterize privacy loss remain open, and existing work is further limited by convergence analyses that rely on restrictive convexity assumptions or ignore the effect of gradient clipping. To overcome these issues, we present a comprehensive analysis of privacy and convergence for DPWFL with general smooth non-convex loss objectives. Our analysis explicitly incorporates both device selection and mini-batch sampling, and shows that the privacy loss can converge to a constant rather than diverge with the number of iterations. Moreover, we establish convergence guarantees with gradient clipping and derive an explicit privacy-utility trade-off. Numerical results validate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [166] [Communication-Efficient and Robust Multi-Modal Federated Learning via Latent-Space Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19067)
*Mohamed Badi,Chaouki Ben Issaid,Mehdi Bennis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoMFed是一个通信高效的多模态联邦学习框架，使用可学习投影矩阵生成压缩潜在表示，通过潜在空间正则化对齐客户端表示，提高跨模态一致性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在多模态设置中面临挑战：客户端通常具有异构模态和模型架构，难以在保护隐私和最小化通信成本的同时高效对齐特征空间

Method: 引入CoMFed框架，使用可学习投影矩阵生成压缩潜在表示，通过潜在空间正则化对齐客户端表示，提高跨模态一致性和对异常值的鲁棒性

Result: 在人类活动识别基准测试中，CoMFed以最小开销实现了具有竞争力的准确性

Conclusion: CoMFed为多模态联邦学习提供了一种通信高效的解决方案，能够有效处理客户端异构性并保持隐私保护

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data, but applying FL to multi-modal settings introduces significant challenges. Clients typically possess heterogeneous modalities and model architectures, making it difficult to align feature spaces efficiently while preserving privacy and minimizing communication costs. To address this, we introduce CoMFed, a Communication-Efficient Multi-Modal Federated Learning framework that uses learnable projection matrices to generate compressed latent representations. A latent-space regularizer aligns these representations across clients, improving cross-modal consistency and robustness to outliers. Experiments on human activity recognition benchmarks show that CoMFed achieves competitive accuracy with minimal overhead.

</details>


### [167] [On Optimizing Multimodal Jailbreaks for Spoken Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19127)
*Aravind Krishnan,Karolina Stańczak,Dietrich Klakow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JAMA：针对语音语言模型的多模态越狱攻击框架，通过联合优化文本和音频模态，显著提升攻击成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音语言模型（SLMs）继承了LLM的安全漏洞并扩大了攻击面，现有攻击多为单模态（仅文本或仅音频），需要探索多模态联合攻击以评估SLMs的鲁棒性

Method: 提出JAMA框架，结合Greedy Coordinate Gradient（GCG）优化文本和Projected Gradient Descent（PGD）优化音频，同时扰动两种模态；还提出顺序近似方法加速攻击

Result: 在4个SOTA SLMs和4种音频类型上评估，JAMA比单模态越狱成功率提高1.5倍到10倍；顺序近似方法使攻击速度提升4-6倍

Conclusion: 单模态安全性不足以确保SLMs的鲁棒性，多模态联合攻击揭示了SLMs的新安全漏洞，需要开发多模态安全防御机制

Abstract: As Spoken Language Models (SLMs) integrate speech and text modalities, they inherit the safety vulnerabilities of their LLM backbone and an expanded attack surface. SLMs have been previously shown to be susceptible to jailbreaking, where adversarial prompts induce harmful responses. Yet existing attacks largely remain unimodal, optimizing either text or audio in isolation. We explore gradient-based multimodal jailbreaks by introducing JAMA (Joint Audio-text Multimodal Attack), a joint multimodal optimization framework combining Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG) for text and Projected Gradient Descent (PGD) for audio, to simultaneously perturb both modalities. Evaluations across four state-of-the-art SLMs and four audio types demonstrate that JAMA surpasses unimodal jailbreak rate by 1.5x to 10x. We analyze the operational dynamics of this joint attack and show that a sequential approximation method makes it 4x to 6x faster. Our findings suggest that unimodal safety is insufficient for robust SLMs. The code and data are available at https://repos.lsv.uni-saarland.de/akrishnan/multimodal-jailbreak-slm

</details>


### [168] [From Inference Efficiency to Embodied Efficiency: Revisiting Efficiency Metrics for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19131)
*Zhuofan Li,Hongkun Yang,Zhenyang Chen,Yangxuan Chen,Yingyan,Lin,Chaojian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现当前VLA模型研究中基于参数、FLOPs或token解码吞吐量的"效率"概念不能反映机器人平台上的实际性能，真实执行效率由系统级具身行为决定，需要采用新的具身效率评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA研究中普遍使用的效率指标（参数数量、FLOPs、token解码吞吐量）不能准确反映模型在真实机器人平台上的实际性能，需要建立能反映系统级具身行为的效率评估体系。

Method: 通过模型压缩、token稀疏化和动作序列压缩等控制性研究，分析不同方法对传统效率指标和系统级具身效率指标（任务完成时间、轨迹平滑度、累积关节旋转、运动能量等）的影响。

Result: 发现三个关键观察：(1)降低传统计算指标的方法常增加端到端执行成本或降低运动质量；(2)系统级具身效率指标能揭示传统评估中隐藏的动作策略性能差异；(3)常见的适应方法（上下文提示、监督微调）对具身效率的改善有限且存在权衡。

Conclusion: 传统推理效率指标会忽略具身执行的重要方面，纳入具身效率指标能提供更完整的策略行为视图和实际性能评估，实现更公平全面的VLA模型比较。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently enabled embodied agents to perform increasingly complex tasks by jointly reasoning over visual, linguistic, and motor modalities. However, we find that the prevailing notion of ``efficiency'' in current VLA research, characterized by parameters, FLOPs, or token decoding throughput, does not reflect actual performance on robotic platforms. In real-world execution, efficiency is determined by system-level embodied behaviors such as task completion time, trajectory smoothness, cumulative joint rotation, and motion energy. Through controlled studies across model compression, token sparsification, and action sequence compression, we make several observations that challenge common assumptions. (1) Methods that reduce computation under conventional metrics often increase end-to-end execution cost or degrade motion quality, despite maintaining task success rates. (2) System-level embodied efficiency metrics reveal performance differences in the learned action policies that remain hidden under conventional evaluations. (3) Common adaptation methods such as in-context prompting or supervised fine-tuning show only mild and metric-specific improvements in embodied efficiency. While these methods can reduce targeted embodied-efficiency metrics such as jerk or action rate, the resulting gains may come with trade-offs in other metrics, such as longer completion time. Taken together, our results suggest that conventional inference efficiency metrics can overlook important aspects of embodied execution. Incorporating embodied efficiency provides a more complete view of policy behavior and practical performance, enabling fairer and more comprehensive comparisons of VLA models.

</details>


### [169] [Hierarchical Latent Structure Learning through Online Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19139)
*Ines Aitsahalia,Kiyohito Iigaya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HOLMES模型：一种通过在线推理学习层次潜在结构的计算框架，结合嵌套中餐过程先验和序列蒙特卡洛推理，在序列数据中发现层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习系统需要在经验泛化和任务相关细节区分之间取得平衡。现有在线潜在原因模型仅支持扁平分区，而层次贝叶斯模型虽能捕捉多级结构但通常需要离线推理，缺乏同时支持层次结构学习和在线推理的框架。

Method: 提出HOLMES模型，结合嵌套中餐过程先验的变体和序列蒙特卡洛推理，实现无需显式监督的层次潜在表示在线推理。该框架支持试次级别的层次潜在结构推断。

Result: 在模拟中，HOLMES与扁平模型预测性能相当，但学习到更紧凑的表示，支持向高层潜在类别的一次性迁移。在具有嵌套时间结构的上下文依赖任务中，HOLMES相比扁平模型改进了结果预测。

Conclusion: HOLMES提供了一个可处理的在线计算框架，用于发现序列数据中的层次结构，平衡了泛化和区分需求，支持更高效的层次表示学习。

Abstract: Learning systems must balance generalization across experiences with discrimination of task-relevant details. Effective learning therefore requires representations that support both. Online latent-cause models support incremental inference but assume flat partitions, whereas hierarchical Bayesian models capture multilevel structure but typically require offline inference. We introduce the Hierarchical Online Learning of Multiscale Experience Structure (HOLMES) model, a computational framework for hierarchical latent structure learning through online inference. HOLMES combines a variation on the nested Chinese Restaurant Process prior with sequential Monte Carlo inference to perform tractable trial-by-trial inference over hierarchical latent representations without explicit supervision over the latent structure. In simulations, HOLMES matched the predictive performance of flat models while learning more compact representations that supported one-shot transfer to higher-level latent categories. In a context-dependent task with nested temporal structure, HOLMES also improved outcome prediction relative to flat models. These results provide a tractable computational framework for discovering hierarchical structure in sequential data.

</details>


### [170] [SHAPCA: Consistent and Interpretable Explanations for Machine Learning Models on Spectroscopy Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19141)
*Mingxing Zhang,Nicola Rossberg,Simone Innocente,Katarzyna Komolibus,Rekha Gautam,Barry O'Sullivan,Luca Longo,Andrea Visentin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SHAPCA框架，结合PCA降维和SHAP解释，为高维光谱数据提供可解释的机器学习分析，解决特征重要性不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光谱数据的高维度和强共线性给机器学习模型的可解释性带来挑战，导致特征重要性不稳定，影响在临床和安全关键场景中的可信度。

Method: 提出SHAPCA框架：先使用主成分分析(PCA)进行降维，然后应用SHAP进行事后解释，最终将解释映射回原始输入空间，支持全局和局部分析。

Result: 数值分析表明该方法提高了结果的可解释性，并在不同训练运行中表现出更好的一致性。

Conclusion: SHAPCA框架有效解决了光谱数据可解释性问题，使从业者能够将预测与原始信号和生物组分联系起来，增强了模型在关键应用中的可信度。

Abstract: In recent years, machine learning models have been increasingly applied to spectroscopic datasets for chemical and biomedical analysis. For their successful adoption, particularly in clinical and safety-critical settings, professionals and researchers must be able to understand and trust the reasoning behind model predictions. However, the inherently high dimensionality and strong collinearity of spectroscopy data pose a fundamental challenge to model explainability. These properties not only complicate model training but also undermine the stability and consistency of explanations, leading to fluctuations in feature importance across repeated training runs. Feature extraction techniques have been used to reduce the input dimensionality; these new features hinder the connection between the prediction and the original signal. This study proposes SHAPCA, an explainable machine learning pipeline that combines Principal Component Analysis (for dimensionality reduction) and Shapely Additive exPlanations (for post hoc explanation) to provide explanations in the original input space, which a practitioner can interpret and link back to the biological components. The proposed framework enables analysis from both global and local perspectives, revealing the spectral bands that drive overall model behaviour as well as the instance-specific features that influence individual predictions. Numerical analysis demonstrated the interpretability of the results and greater consistency across different runs.

</details>


### [171] [DyMoE: Dynamic Expert Orchestration with Mixed-Precision Quantization for Efficient MoE Inference on Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19172)
*Yuegui Huang,Zhiyuan Fang,Weiqi Luo,Ruoyu Wu,Wuhui Chen,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DyMoE是一个动态混合精度量化框架，专门针对边缘设备上的MoE模型推理优化，通过重要性感知优先级、深度自适应调度和前瞻预取技术，显著降低内存占用和I/O开销，实现实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在边缘平台实时推理面临内存占用大和I/O开销高的挑战，现有静态方法存在固定的延迟-精度权衡问题。研究发现专家重要性存在高度偏斜和深度依赖性，这为动态优化提供了机会。

Method: DyMoE采用三个核心技术：1) 重要性感知优先级：运行时动态量化专家；2) 深度自适应调度：保护关键层的语义完整性；3) 前瞻预取：重叠I/O延迟。这些方法基于专家重要性偏斜和深度依赖敏感性的观察。

Result: 在商用边缘硬件上，DyMoE相比最先进的卸载基线，将首词生成时间(TTFT)减少3.44-22.7倍，每词输出时间(TPOT)加速最高达14.58倍，实现了资源受限边缘设备上的实时、精度保持的MoE推理。

Conclusion: DyMoE通过动态混合精度量化有效解决了MoE模型在边缘设备上的内存和I/O瓶颈，实现了实时推理而不牺牲精度，为资源受限环境中的大型模型部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Despite the computational efficiency of MoE models, the excessive memory footprint and I/O overhead inherent in multi-expert architectures pose formidable challenges for real-time inference on resource-constrained edge platforms. While existing static methods struggle with a rigid latency-accuracy trade-off, we observe that expert importance is highly skewed and depth-dependent. Motivated by these insights, we propose DyMoE, a dynamic mixed-precision quantization framework designed for high-performance edge inference. Leveraging insights into expert importance skewness and depth-dependent sensitivity, DyMoE introduces: (1) importance-aware prioritization to dynamically quantize experts at runtime; (2) depth-adaptive scheduling to preserve semantic integrity in critical layers; and (3) look-ahead prefetching to overlap I/O stalls. Experimental results on commercial edge hardware show that DyMoE reduces Time-to-First-Token (TTFT) by 3.44x-22.7x and up to a 14.58x speedup in Time-Per-Output-Token (TPOT) compared to state-of-the-art offloading baselines, enabling real-time, accuracy-preserving MoE inference on resource-constrained edge devices.

</details>


### [172] [SOL-ExecBench: Speed-of-Light Benchmarking for Real-World GPU Kernels Against Hardware Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19173)
*Edward Lin,Sahil Modi,Siva Kumar Sastry Hari,Qijing Huang,Zhifan Ye,Nestor Qin,Fengzhe Zhou,Yuan Zhang,Jingquan Wang,Sana Damani,Dheeraj Peri,Ouye Xie,Aditya Kane,Moshe Maor,Michael Behar,Triston Cao,Rishabh Mehta,Vartika Singh,Vikram Sharma Mailthody,Terry Chen,Zihao Ye,Hanfeng Chen,Tianqi Chen,Vinod Grover,Wei Chen,Wei Liu,Eric Chung,Luis Ceze,Roger Bringmann,Cyril Zeller,Michael Lightstone,Christos Kozyrakis,Humphrey Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOL-ExecBench是一个包含235个CUDA内核优化问题的基准测试，针对NVIDIA Blackwell GPU，通过硬件速度极限(SOL)边界而非软件基线来评估内核性能，提供硬件效率导向的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于软件基线评估AI系统生成GPU内核的方法存在局限性，无法准确衡量硬件效率。需要建立以硬件速度极限为目标的基准测试，推动内核优化向硬件效率方向发展。

Method: 从124个生产和新兴AI模型中提取235个CUDA内核优化问题，覆盖多种数据类型和工作负载。开发SOLAR管道计算硬件速度极限边界，提供SOL分数量化内核性能与硬件极限的接近程度。建立沙盒化测试环境防止奖励黑客策略。

Result: 创建了SOL-ExecBench基准测试套件，包含235个针对Blackwell GPU的内核优化问题，支持BF16、FP8和NVFP4数据类型。提供了硬件速度极限边界计算方法和SOL评分系统，以及防止作弊的测试环境。

Conclusion: SOL-ExecBench将GPU内核基准测试从超越可变软件基线的范式转变为缩小与硬件速度极限差距的新范式，为评估智能优化系统提供了更准确、硬件效率导向的评估框架。

Abstract: As agentic AI systems become increasingly capable of generating and optimizing GPU kernels, progress is constrained by benchmarks that reward speedup over software baselines rather than proximity to hardware-efficient execution. We present SOL-ExecBench, a benchmark of 235 CUDA kernel optimization problems extracted from 124 production and emerging AI models spanning language, diffusion, vision, audio, video, and hybrid architectures, targeting NVIDIA Blackwell GPUs. The benchmark covers forward and backward workloads across BF16, FP8, and NVFP4, including kernels whose best performance is expected to rely on Blackwell-specific capabilities. Unlike prior benchmarks that evaluate kernels primarily relative to software implementations, SOL-ExecBench measures performance against analytically derived Speed-of-Light (SOL) bounds computed by SOLAR, our pipeline for deriving hardware-grounded SOL bounds, yielding a fixed target for hardware-efficient optimization. We report a SOL Score that quantifies how much of the gap between a release-defined scoring baseline and the hardware SOL bound a candidate kernel closes. To support robust evaluation of agentic optimizers, we additionally provide a sandboxed harness with GPU clock locking, L2 cache clearing, isolated subprocess execution, and static analysis based checks against common reward-hacking strategies. SOL-ExecBench reframes GPU kernel benchmarking from beating a mutable software baseline to closing the remaining gap to hardware Speed-of-Light.

</details>


### [173] [MIDST Challenge at SaTML 2025: Membership Inference over Diffusion-models-based Synthetic Tabular data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19185)
*Masoumeh Shafieinejad,Xi He,Mahshid Alinoori,John Jewell,Sana Ayromlou,Wei Pang,Veronica Chatrath,Garui Sharma,Deval Pandya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MIDST挑战赛评估扩散模型生成的合成表格数据在成员推理攻击下的隐私保护效果，开发了针对扩散模型的黑盒和白盒攻击方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据常被视为数据匿名化和隐私保护数据发布的理想解决方案，但扩散模型生成的表格数据在隐私攻击（特别是成员推理攻击）下的实际保护效果尚未得到充分探索。

Method: MIDST挑战赛对扩散模型生成的合成表格数据进行定量评估，重点关注其对成员推理攻击的抵抗能力。针对表格数据的异质性和复杂性，探索了多种目标模型，包括混合数据类型的单表扩散模型和具有互连约束的多关系表扩散模型。开发了专门针对这些扩散模型的黑盒和白盒成员推理攻击方法。

Result: MIDST挑战赛开发了针对扩散模型的新型黑盒和白盒成员推理攻击方法，能够全面评估扩散模型生成合成表格数据的隐私保护效果。相关代码已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: 扩散模型生成的合成表格数据在隐私保护方面的实际效果需要通过专门的评估方法来验证，MIDST挑战赛为此提供了系统性的评估框架和攻击方法，有助于推动隐私保护合成数据生成技术的发展。

Abstract: Synthetic data is often perceived as a silver-bullet solution to data anonymization and privacy-preserving data publishing. Drawn from generative models like diffusion models, synthetic data is expected to preserve the statistical properties of the original dataset while remaining resilient to privacy attacks. Recent developments of diffusion models have been effective on a wide range of data types, but their privacy resilience, particularly for tabular formats, remains largely unexplored. MIDST challenge sought a quantitative evaluation of the privacy gain of synthetic tabular data generated by diffusion models, with a specific focus on its resistance to membership inference attacks (MIAs). Given the heterogeneity and complexity of tabular data, multiple target models were explored for MIAs, including diffusion models for single tables of mixed data types and multi-relational tables with interconnected constraints. MIDST inspired the development of novel black-box and white-box MIAs tailored to these target diffusion models as a key outcome, enabling a comprehensive evaluation of their privacy efficacy. The MIDST GitHub repository is available at https://github.com/VectorInstitute/MIDST

</details>


### [174] [Improving RCT-Based Treatment Effect Estimation Under Covariate Mismatch via Calibrated Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19186)
*Amir Asiaee,Samhita Pal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CALM方法通过嵌入对齐解决RCT与观察性研究间的协变量不匹配问题，避免插补，在非线性CATE估计中表现优异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机对照试验(RCTs)是估计异质性治疗效应的金标准，但通常对检测效应异质性缺乏统计功效。大型观察性研究(OS)可以补充RCTs进行条件平均治疗效应(CATE)估计，但关键障碍是协变量不匹配：两种来源测量不同且仅部分重叠的协变量。

Method: 提出CALM(Calibrated ALignment under covariate Mismatch)方法，通过学习嵌入将每个数据源的特征映射到共同的表示空间，绕过插补。将OS结果模型转移到RCT嵌入空间，并使用试验数据进行校准，保持随机化带来的因果识别性。

Result: 有限样本风险界限分解为对齐误差、结果模型复杂度和校准复杂度项，识别了嵌入对齐何时优于插补。在线性变体中提供对负迁移的保护；在稀疏线性模型下，嵌入方法严格泛化了插补。51个设置的模拟证实：(i)基于校准的方法在线性CATE中效果相当；(ii)神经嵌入变体在所有22个非线性设置中均以较大优势胜出。

Conclusion: CALM通过嵌入对齐有效解决了RCT与观察性研究间的协变量不匹配问题，在非线性CATE估计中显著优于传统方法，为结合两种数据源进行异质性治疗效应估计提供了有前景的框架。

Abstract: Randomized controlled trials (RCTs) are the gold standard for estimating heterogeneous treatment effects, yet they are often underpowered for detecting effect heterogeneity. Large observational studies (OS) can supplement RCTs for conditional average treatment effect (CATE) estimation, but a key barrier is covariate mismatch: the two sources measure different, only partially overlapping, covariates. We propose CALM (Calibrated ALignment under covariate Mismatch), which bypasses imputation by learning embeddings that map each source's features into a common representation space. OS outcome models are transferred to the RCT embedding space and calibrated using trial data, preserving causal identification from randomization. Finite-sample risk bounds decompose into alignment error, outcome-model complexity, and calibration complexity terms, identifying when embedding alignment outperforms imputation. Under the calibration-based linear variant, the framework provides protection against negative transfer; the neural variant can be vulnerable under severe distributional shift. Under sparse linear models, the embedding approach strictly generalizes imputation. Simulations across 51 settings confirm that (i) calibration-based methods are equivalent for linear CATEs, and (ii) the neural embedding variant wins all 22 nonlinear-regime settings with large margins.

</details>


### [175] [Robustness, Cost, and Attack-Surface Concentration in Phishing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19204)
*Julian Allagan,Mohamed Elbakary,Zohreh Safari,Weizheng Gao,Gabrielle Morgan,Essence Morgan,Vladimir Deriglazov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了钓鱼网站检测器在对抗性特征操纵下的鲁棒性，提出了基于成本感知的规避框架和三个诊断指标，发现模型鲁棒性主要受特征经济性而非模型复杂度影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有钓鱼检测器在独立同分布评估中准确率接近完美，但实际部署安全取决于对部署后特征操纵的鲁棒性。研究旨在通过成本感知规避框架来量化这一差距。

Method: 提出成本感知规避框架，建模离散单调特征编辑和显式攻击者预算。引入三个诊断指标：最小规避成本(MEC)、规避生存率S(B)和鲁棒性集中指数(RCI)。在UCI钓鱼网站基准(11,055个实例，30个三元特征)上评估了逻辑回归、随机森林、梯度提升树和XGBoost等模型。

Result: 所有模型在静态评估中AUC≥0.979，但在预算化规避下鲁棒性趋于一致：全特征时中位MEC为2，超过80%的最小成本规避集中在三个低成本表面特征。特征限制仅在移除所有主导低成本转换时改善鲁棒性。严格成本计划下，基础设施特征集对集成模型有17-19%不可行质量，而可规避实例的中位MEC保持不变。

Conclusion: 钓鱼检测的对抗鲁棒性主要由特征经济性而非模型复杂度决定。如果正比例的钓鱼实例可通过单个最小成本特征转换规避，则任何分类器都无法在不修改特征表示或成本模型的情况下提高相应的MEC分位数。

Abstract: Phishing detectors built on engineered website features attain near-perfect accuracy under i.i.d.\ evaluation, yet deployment security depends on robustness to post-deployment feature manipulation. We study this gap through a cost-aware evasion framework that models discrete, monotone feature edits under explicit attacker budgets. Three diagnostics are introduced: minimal evasion cost (MEC), the evasion survival rate $S(B)$, and the robustness concentration index (RCI).
  On the UCI Phishing Websites benchmark (11\,055 instances, 30 ternary features), Logistic Regression, Random Forests, Gradient Boosted Trees, and XGBoost all achieve $\mathrm{AUC}\ge 0.979$ under static evaluation. Under budgeted sanitization-style evasion, robustness converges across architectures: the median MEC equals 2 with full features, and over 80\% of successful minimal-cost evasions concentrate on three low-cost surface features. Feature restriction improves robustness only when it removes all dominant low-cost transitions. Under strict cost schedules, infrastructure-leaning feature sets exhibit 17-19\% infeasible mass for ensemble models, while the median MEC among evadable instances remains unchanged. We formalize this convergence: if a positive fraction of correctly detected phishing instances admit evasion through a single feature transition of minimal cost $c_{\min}$, no classifier can raise the corresponding MEC quantile above $c_{\min}$ without modifying the feature representation or cost model. Adversarial robustness in phishing detection is governed by feature economics rather than model complexity.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Using Laplace Transform To Optimize the Hallucination of Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18022)
*Cheng Kang,Xinye Chen,Daniel Novak,Xujing Yao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文从控制理论视角将生成模型形式化为随机动力系统，分析其幻觉问题，提出通过拉普拉斯变换分析和模拟源响应来优化生成模型的幻觉问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索避免生成模型产生自信错误（幻觉）的可行性，从控制理论角度分析生成模型的系统功能，为解决幻觉问题提供理论框架

Method: 将生成模型形式化为随机动力系统，运用控制理论分析系统函数和系统响应，采用拉普拉斯变换分析，模拟源响应来应对高复杂度优化问题

Result: 发现训练进度与相应系统响应一致，这为开发更好的优化组件提供了有用途径，最终通过拉普拉斯变换分析从根本上优化了生成模型的幻觉问题

Conclusion: 控制理论为分析生成模型幻觉问题提供了有效框架，拉普拉斯变换分析和源响应模拟是解决生成模型幻觉问题的可行方法，训练进度与系统响应的关联性为优化设计提供了指导

Abstract: To explore the feasibility of avoiding the confident error (or hallucination) of generation models (GMs), we formalise the system of GMs as a class of stochastic dynamical systems through the lens of control theory. Numerous factors can be attributed to the hallucination of the learning process of GMs, utilising knowledge of control theory allows us to analyse their system functions and system responses. Due to the high complexity of GMs when using various optimization methods, we cannot figure out their solution of Laplace transform, but from a macroscopic perspective, simulating the source response provides a virtual way to address the hallucination of GMs. We also find that the training progress is consistent with the corresponding system response, which offers us a useful way to develop a better optimization component. Finally, the hallucination problem of GMs is fundamentally optimized by using Laplace transform analysis.

</details>


### [177] [A Hybrid Decomposition Approach for Stochastic Unit Commitment with Combined-Cycle Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18177)
*Rosemary Barrass,Harsha Nagarajan,Mathieu Tanneau,Russell Bent,Pascal Van Hentenryck*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合Benders分解和Dantzig-Wolfe分解算法，用于解决包含联合循环发电机的随机机组组合问题，显著提高了求解速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国电网正经历重大范式转变，可再生能源、电动汽车和数据中心的发展增加了对快速响应发电能力的需求。联合循环发电机因其高效率而得到广泛应用，但在机组组合问题中引入了大量二元约束和变量，增加了求解复杂度。

Method: 提出了一种混合Benders分解和Dantzig-Wolfe分解算法：利用Benders分解处理机组组合问题中的线性约束可分离性，利用Dantzig-Wolfe分解处理联合循环发电机的整数约束。

Result: 在修改后的935台发电机FERC测试数据集上进行验证，算法在所有情况下都比传统Benders分解显著加速。在25个或更多场景的情况下，比Benders分解和Gurobi分支定界求解器具有更好的收敛速度。

Conclusion: 该算法是解决随机机组组合问题的可扩展方法，特别适用于包含联合循环发电机的大规模复杂电网优化问题。

Abstract: The U.S. power grid is undergoing a major paradigm shift with the increased development of renewable generators, electric vehicles, and data centers. In response to this growing need, the U.S. has ramped up the construction of combined-cycle generators (CCs). CCs are fast-ramping generators that utilize variable configurations of combustion turbines (CTs) and steam turbines (STs) to achieve much higher efficiency than traditional CTs alone. For schedule optimization, this requires the addition of a large number of binary constraints and variables in Unit Commitment (UC) problem formulations. This paper presents a novel hybrid Benders' (BD) and Dantzig-Wolfe (DW) decomposition algorithm for stochastic UC problems with CCs. The algorithm exploits the separability of the linear constraints in UC through BD and the integer CC constraints through DW. Results are presented for the 935-generator FERC test data set, modified to include mode data for CCs. The algorithm demonstrates a significant speed-up over traditional BD across all cases. It also demonstrates better convergence rates on cases with 25 or more scenarios than both BD and Gurobi's branch-and-bound solver. These cases show that the proposed algorithm is a scalable approach for solving stochastic UC.

</details>


### [178] [Solving Sparsity Constrained PCA, Regression, and QCQP via the Spartrahedron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18215)
*Diego Cifuentes,Zhuorui Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于新型凸锥（spartrahedron）的稀疏二次约束二次规划（QCQP）凸松弛框架，该框架能精确刻画矩阵层面的稀疏性，并通过半定规划松弛在秩一解时提供全局最优性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏性在统计学、信号处理和数据科学中是基本建模原则，但带有稀疏约束的优化问题通常难以求解。现有方法在处理稀疏二次约束二次规划（如稀疏回归、稀疏主成分分析等）时存在困难，需要新的凸松弛框架来有效解决这类问题。

Method: 引入新型凸锥"spartrahedron"，该锥能精确刻画矩阵层面的稀疏性。基于此构建稀疏QCQP的半定规划（SDP）松弛，当松弛解为秩一时，该松弛是紧的，从而提供全局最优性的简单证明。方法适用于稀疏回归、稀疏PCA等问题的统一处理。

Result: 建立了理论保证，包括稀疏PCA和稀疏岭回归的近似界和精确性区域，以及扰动下的稳定性结果。数值实验在稀疏PCA、稀疏回归、RIP常数估计和稀疏典型相关分析（CCA）上验证了方法的实际效果。

Conclusion: 提出的基于spartrahedron的凸松弛框架为稀疏QCQP问题提供了有效的解决方案，具有理论保证和实际应用价值，能统一处理多种稀疏优化问题并证明全局最优性。

Abstract: Sparsity is a fundamental modeling principle in statistics, signal processing, and data science. However, optimization with sparsity constraints is notoriously difficult. We introduce a new convex relaxation framework for {sparse quadratically constrained quadratic programs} (QCQPs), a class that subsumes sparse regression, sparse principal component analysis (PCA), and related problems. Our approach is based on a novel convex cone, the spartrahedron, which exactly characterizes sparsity at the matrix level. This leads to a semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxation that is tight whenever its solution is rank-one, providing a simple certificate of global optimality. We establish theoretical guarantees, including approximation bounds and exactness regions for sparse PCA and sparse ridge regression, as well as a general stability result under perturbations. Numerical experiments on sparse PCA, sparse regression, RIP constant estimation, and sparse canonical correlation analysis (CCA) demonstrate the practical success of our methods.

</details>


### [179] [Forward-Backward Dynamic Programming for LQG Dynamic Games with Partial and Asymmetric Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18304)
*Yuxiang Guan,Iman Shames,Tyler Summers*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于具有部分和不对称信息的两人零和随机动态博弈的算法，通过限制智能体内部状态维度避免高阶信念无限回归，在线性二次高斯框架下实现了可计算均衡策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息不对称在随机动态博弈中引入了根本性挑战，涉及信念表示和心智理论问题，智能体需要推断其他智能体的信念状态和估计来制定策略。传统方法面临高阶信念无限回归问题，导致计算不可行。

Method: 采用线性二次高斯模型，考虑具有有限内部状态维度的策略。提出了两种算法：1）有限时域问题的迭代前向-后向算法，联合计算信念状态、均衡策略和价值函数；2）平均成本无限时域问题的类价值迭代算法，计算稳态信念状态和均衡策略。

Result: 开发了算法的开源实现，并通过数值实验证明了所提算法的有效性。算法能够处理信息不对称带来的挑战，避免了高阶信念无限回归问题，实现了可计算的均衡策略。

Conclusion: 通过限制智能体内部状态维度，在线性二次高斯框架下成功解决了具有部分和不对称信息的随机动态博弈问题，提出了可计算的算法并验证了其有效性，为信息不对称博弈提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We formulate and study a class of two-player zero-sum stochastic dynamic games with partial and asymmetric information. Information asymmetry introduces fundamental challenges involving \emph{belief representation} and \emph{theory of mind} issues, where agents must impute belief states and estimates of other agents to inform their own strategy. To avoid an infinite regress of higher-order beliefs amongst agents and obtain computationally implementable results, we focus on a linear quadratic Gaussian (LQG) model and consider strategies with limited internal state dimension. We present a novel iterative forward-backward algorithm to jointly compute belief states and equilibrium strategies and value functions for a finite-horizon problem. We also present a value iteration-like algorithm to jointly compute stationary belief states and equilibrium strategies for an average-cost infinite-horizon problem. An open-source implementation of the algorithms is provided, and we demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithms in numerical experiments.

</details>


### [180] [Adversarial Robustness for Matrix Control Barrier Functions in Sampled-Data Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18307)
*James Usevitch*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了在采样数据系统中使用矩阵控制屏障函数保证多智能体集合不变性的新理论结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多智能体系统在采样数据环境下的安全性问题，需要解决异构控制仿射智能体在零阶保持控制输入下的集合不变性保证，同时考虑对抗性智能体试图将系统状态驱动到不安全集合的情况

Method: 1. 提出矩阵控制屏障函数方法，为异构控制仿射智能体在零阶保持控制输入下计算控制输入，使矩阵不等式定义的安全集合保持前向不变；2. 引入考虑对抗性智能体存在的集合不变性保证方法；3. 将集合不变性结果扩展到相对于矩阵值安全集函数具有高相对度的系统

Result: 建立了保证多智能体集合不变性的理论条件，包括：1. 异构智能体在采样数据系统中的安全控制条件；2. 对抗性智能体存在时的集合不变性保证方法；3. 高相对度系统的理论扩展结果

Conclusion: 该论文为多智能体采样数据系统提供了基于矩阵控制屏障函数的集合不变性理论框架，能够处理异构智能体、对抗性智能体以及高相对度系统等复杂情况，增强了多智能体系统的安全保证能力

Abstract: This paper presents novel theoretical results to guarantee multi-agent set invariance using Matrix Control Barrier Functions in sampled-data systems. More specifically, the paper presents conditions under which heterogeneous control-affine agents applying zero-order-hold control inputs can compute control inputs to render safe sets defined by matrix inequalities forward invariant. It then introduces methods to guarantee set invariance while accounting for the presence of adversarial agents seeking to drive the system state to unsafe sets. Finally, the paper presents theoretical extensions of these set invariance results to systems having high relative degree with respect to the matrix-valued safe set function.

</details>


### [181] [Stabilization of highly nonlinear hybrid stochastic differential delay equations by periodically intermittent feedback controls based on discrete-time observations with asynchronous switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18367)
*Guangqiang Lan,Fansai Meng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究高度非线性混合随机时滞微分方程的矩指数稳定化，设计基于离散时间状态观测的周期间歇控制器，获得观测周期上界和控制宽度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高度非线性混合随机时滞微分方程，需要设计有效的控制器来实现系统的指数稳定。传统连续时间控制器在实际应用中可能不切实际，因此考虑基于离散时间状态观测的周期间歇控制器，同时处理异步切换问题。

Method: 设计基于离散时间状态观测的周期间歇控制器，采用异步切换策略。首先在广义Khasminskii型条件下建立解p阶矩的有穷性和有界性，然后给出控制函数、漂移系数和扩散系数的合理条件，证明受控系统的指数稳定性及收敛速率。

Result: 获得了观测周期的上界和控制宽度的下界，证明了受控系统的指数稳定性，并确定了收敛速率。通过示例表明控制区间比例与收敛速率呈正相关关系。

Conclusion: 提出的基于离散时间状态观测的周期间歇控制器能有效实现高度非线性混合随机时滞微分方程的矩指数稳定，控制区间比例越大收敛越快，为实际工程应用提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we will investigate the moment exponential stabilization of highly nonlinear hybrid stochastic differential delay equations. A periodically intermittent controller based on discrete time state observations with asynchronous switching is designed. The upper bound of observation period as well as the lower bound of the control width are all obtained. Firstly, the finiteness and boundedness of the $p$-th moment of the solution are established under a generalized Khasminskii-type condition. Then reasonable conditions of control function, drift and diffusion coefficients are presented. Then exponential stability as well as the convergence rate of controlled system are proved. Finally, an example is presented to interpret the conclusion, which also indicates that the proportion of control interval has positive relation to the convergence rate.

</details>


### [182] [Fundamental Limits for Sensor-Based Control via the Gibbs Variational Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18454)
*Vincent Pacelli,Evangelos A. Theodorou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于吉布斯变分原理的自洽下界方法，用于计算部分观测下因果反馈控制器的最小期望成本，解决了现有信息论方法在反馈最有价值时性能下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反馈控制器性能的基本极限对于算法基准测试、传感器选择和任务可行性认证至关重要，但缺乏通用计算工具。现有信息论方法通过评估传感器相对于非受控系统的性能来高估传感器必须提供的信息，导致在反馈最有价值时界限性能下降。

Method: 通过将吉布斯变分原理应用于状态和观测的联合路径测度，推导出部分观测下任何因果反馈控制器的最小期望成本下界。该方法具有自洽细化特性：好的控制器会集中状态分布，从而限制传感器可提取的信息，进而收紧界限。通过二分法求解固定点方程，并在自由能最小化可证明为凸的条件下提供可验证正确的数值界限。

Result: 在非线性Dubins汽车跟踪问题上，自洽界限在不同传感器噪声水平下捕获了大部分最优成本，而开环变体在低噪声时界限无效。该方法适用于非线性、非完整和混合动力学系统，支持无界成本。

Conclusion: 提出的自洽下界方法为反馈控制器性能提供了更准确的界限，特别是在反馈最有价值的情况下，解决了现有信息论方法的局限性，为控制器设计、传感器选择和任务可行性认证提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Fundamental limits on the performance of feedback controllers are essential for benchmarking algorithms, guiding sensor selection, and certifying task feasibility -- yet few general-purpose tools exist for computing them. Existing information-theoretic approaches overestimate the information a sensor must provide by evaluating it against the uncontrolled system, producing bounds that degrade precisely when feedback is most valuable. We derive a lower bound on the minimum expected cost of any causal feedback controller under partial observations by applying the Gibbs variational principle to the joint path measure over states and observations. The bound applies to nonlinear, nonholonomic, and hybrid dynamics with unbounded costs and admits a self-consistent refinement: any good controller concentrates the state, which limits the information the sensor can extract, which tightens the bound. The resulting fixed-point equation has a unique solution computable by bisection, and we provide conditions under which the free energy minimization is provably convex, yielding a certifiably correct numerical bound. On a nonlinear Dubins car tracking problem, the self-consistent bound captures most of the optimal cost across sensor noise levels, while the open-loop variant is vacuous at low noise.

</details>


### [183] [Axis-Aligned Relaxations for Mixed-Integer Nonlinear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18458)
*Haisheng Zhu,Taotao He,Mohit Tawarmalani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于计算几何的混合整数非线性规划松弛框架，通过凸化策略选择的点集构建多面体松弛，迭代逼近因子函数图的同步凸包，在多项式优化和MINLPLib实例上显著提升对偶界质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合整数非线性规划松弛方法（如因子松弛、复合松弛）在逼近非凸函数图凸包方面存在局限性，需要更精确的松弛技术来改进对偶界质量，加速全局优化求解。

Method: 基于计算几何构建多面体松弛框架：1) 提出函数乘积的新型显式不等式，改进标准因子和复合松弛方案；2) 证明轴对齐区域上多线性函数的同步凸包完全由角点值确定；3) 集成体素化和QuickHull等计算几何工具高效逼近可行域和函数图。

Result: 在随机生成多项式优化问题中，相比标准方法在一半实例上额外闭合20-25%的最优性间隙；在MINLPLib的619个实例上，相比增强因子编程基线和Gurobi根节点界，在约30%实例上获得更优对偶界，约10%实例的间隙减少超过50%。

Conclusion: 该计算几何驱动的松弛框架显著提升了混合整数非线性规划的对偶界质量，为全局优化提供了更有效的松弛技术，证明了将计算几何工具集成到数学规划中的价值。

Abstract: We present a novel relaxation framework for general mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) grounded in computational geometry. Our approach constructs polyhedral relaxations by convexifying finite sets of strategically chosen points, iteratively refining the approximation to converge toward the simultaneous convex hull of factorable function graphs. The framework is underpinned by three key contributions: (i) a new class of explicit inequalities for products of functions that strictly improve upon standard factorable and composite relaxation schemes; (ii) a proof establishing that the simultaneous convex hull of multilinear functions over axis-aligned regions is fully determined by their values at corner points, thereby generalizing existing results from hypercubes to arbitrary axis-aligned domains; and (iii) the integration of computational geometry tools, specifically voxelization and QuickHull, to efficiently approximate feasible regions and function graphs. We implement this framework and evaluate it on randomly generated polynomial optimization problems and a suite of 619 instances from \texttt{MINLPLib}. Numerical results demonstrate significant improvements over state-of-the-art benchmarks: on polynomial instances, our relaxation closes an additional 20--25\% of the optimality gap relative to standard methods on half the instances. Furthermore, compared against an enhanced factorable programming baseline and Gurobi's root-node bounds, our approach yields superior dual bounds on approximately 30\% of \texttt{MINLPLib} instances, with roughly 10\% of cases exhibiting a gap reduction exceeding 50\%.

</details>


### [184] [Modeling Adversarial Wildfires for Power Grid Disruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18473)
*Matthew Brun,Xu Andy Sun,Jean-Paul Watson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种混合整数圆锥规划方法来模拟针对电力基础设施的对抗性野火，基于Rothermel火蔓延模型设计风助火蔓延集，并应用于加州真实火灾案例，以识别最小停电时间和最大负荷削减，用于构建安全约束最优潮流模型以提升电网抗野火能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力基础设施面临日益严重的野火破坏风险，但传统野火模型无法有效描述最坏情况或高影响火灾行为。电网运营商需要评估不可预测火灾的潜在影响，以提升电网的运营韧性。

Method: 提出混合整数圆锥规划方法，基于Rothermel火蔓延模型设计风助火蔓延集，开发新的欧几里得球内积凸松弛技术，构建对抗性野火模型来模拟针对电力基础设施的火灾传播。

Result: 使用加州Park、Eaton和Palisades火灾案例进行测试，识别了多元素故障的最小停电时间和由现实野火引起的元件停运序列相关的最大负荷削减。利用最小停电时间值筛选故障并构建安全约束最优潮流模型。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效表征针对基础设施的对抗性野火，同时尊重现实的火蔓延动态，为电网运营商提供了评估和提升电网抗野火韧性的实用工具，通过安全约束最优潮流模型促进运营韧性。

Abstract: Electric power infrastructure faces increasing risk of damage and disruption due to wildfire. Operators of power grids in wildfire-prone regions must consider the potential impacts of unpredictable fires. However, traditional wildfire models do not effectively describe worst-case, or even high-impact, fire behavior. To address this issue, we propose a mixed-integer conic program to characterize an adversarial wildfire that targets infrastructure while respecting realistic fire spread dynamics. We design a wind-assisted fire spread set based on the Rothermel fire spread model and propose principled convex relaxations of this set, including a new relaxation of the inner product over Euclidean balls. We present test cases derived from the recent Park, Eaton, and Palisades fires in California and solve models to identify the minimum time-to-outage of multiple-element contingencies and the maximum load shed associated with a sequence of element outages caused by a realistic wildfire. We use the minimum time-to-outage values to screen contingencies and construct security-constrained optimal power flow models that promote operational resilience against wildfire.

</details>


### [185] [Computationally Efficient Density-Driven Optimal Control via Analytical KKT Reduction and Contractive MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18503)
*Julian Martinez,Kooktae Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种将多智能体密度驱动最优控制（D2OC）的KKT系统转化为紧凑二次规划的方法，实现线性计算复杂度，显著降低在线计算负担，并证明闭环系统的输入到状态稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密度驱动最优控制（D2OC）框架作为预测控制器需要求解大规模KKT系统，其计算复杂度随预测时域呈立方增长，限制了大规模多智能体系统的长时域预测控制应用。

Method: 提出解析结构约简方法，将T时域的KKT系统转化为紧凑二次规划（QP）形式，实现O(T)线性可扩展性；引入收缩性Lyapunov约束，并证明闭环系统对参考传播漂移的输入到状态稳定性（ISS）。

Result: 数值仿真验证该方法能实现快速密度覆盖，相比传统O(T^3)方法显著提升计算速度，使大规模多智能体集群的长时域预测控制成为可能。

Conclusion: 提出的结构约简方法有效解决了D2OC框架的计算瓶颈，实现了线性计算复杂度的预测控制，为大规模多智能体系统的空间分布协调提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient coordination for collective spatial distribution is a fundamental challenge in multi-agent systems. Prior research on Density-Driven Optimal Control (D2OC) established a framework to match agent trajectories to a desired spatial distribution. However, implementing this as a predictive controller requires solving a large-scale Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) system, whose computational complexity grows cubically with the prediction horizon. To resolve this, we propose an analytical structural reduction that transforms the T-horizon KKT system into a condensed quadratic program (QP). This formulation achieves O(T) linear scalability, significantly reducing the online computational burden compared to conventional O(T^3) approaches. Furthermore, to ensure rigorous convergence in dynamic environments, we incorporate a contractive Lyapunov constraint and prove the Input-to-State Stability (ISS) of the closed-loop system against reference propagation drift. Numerical simulations verify that the proposed method facilitates rapid density coverage with substantial computational speed-up, enabling long-horizon predictive control for large-scale multi-agent swarms.

</details>


### [186] [Learning Decision-Sufficient Representations for Linear Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18551)
*Yuhan Ye,Saurabh Amin,Asuman Ozdaglar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了线性规划中决策充分数据集的构造问题，证明了相关计算问题的NP-hard性，提出了多项式时间的近似算法，并获得了分布无关的PAC保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何构建压缩的数据集，使其能够恢复线性规划的最优决策，其中成本向量c来自先验集合C但未知。Bennouna等人的工作提供了决策充分数据集(SDDs)的几何特征，但其构造算法需要求解混合整数规划，计算复杂度高。

Method: 1. 证明计算决策相关维度d*是NP-hard问题，判断数据集是否全局充分是coNP-hard问题；2. 引入逐点充分性(pointwise sufficiency)作为松弛条件；3. 在非退化假设下，提出多项式时间的割平面算法构建逐点充分决策数据集；4. 在i.i.d.成本数据驱动场景中，提出累积算法聚合决策相关方向；5. 将决策充分表示应用于上下文线性优化。

Result: 1. 建立了计算d*的NP-hard性和判断全局充分性的coNP-hard性；2. 提出了多项式时间算法构建逐点充分数据集；3. 获得了分布无关的PAC保证：在训练样本上，新样本的逐点充分性失败概率最多为Õ(d*/n)，该速率在对数因子内是紧的；4. 在上下文线性优化中，获得了压缩预测器的泛化界，尺度为Õ(√(d*/n))而非Õ(√(d/n))。

Conclusion: 该论文解决了线性规划中决策充分数据集构造的计算复杂性问题，通过引入逐点充分性概念和多项式时间算法，在保持理论保证的同时显著降低了计算复杂度，为数据驱动的决策优化提供了实用的压缩表示方法。

Abstract: We study how to construct compressed datasets that suffice to recover optimal decisions in linear programs with an unknown cost vector $c$ lying in a prior set $\mathcal{C}$. Recent work by Bennouna et al. provides an exact geometric characterization of sufficient decision datasets (SDDs) via an intrinsic decision-relevant dimension $d^\star$. However, their algorithm for constructing minimum-size SDDs requires solving mixed-integer programs. In this paper, we establish hardness results showing that computing $d^\star$ is NP-hard and deciding whether a dataset is globally sufficient is coNP-hard, thereby resolving a recent open problem posed by Bennouna et al. To address this worst-case intractability, we introduce pointwise sufficiency, a relaxation that requires sufficiency for an individual cost vector. Under nondegeneracy, we provide a polynomial-time cutting-plane algorithm for constructing pointwise-sufficient decision datasets. In a data-driven regime with i.i.d.\ costs, we further propose a cumulative algorithm that aggregates decision-relevant directions across samples, yielding a stable compression scheme of size at most $d^\star$. This leads to a distribution-free PAC guarantee: with high probability over the training sample, the pointwise sufficiency failure probability on a fresh draw is at most $\tilde{O}(d^\star/n)$, and this rate is tight up to logarithmic factors. Finally, we apply decision-sufficient representations to contextual linear optimization, obtaining compressed predictors with generalization bounds scaling as $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{d^\star/n})$ rather than $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{d/n})$, where $d$ is the ambient cost dimension.

</details>


### [187] [Robust mean-field games under entropy-based uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18628)
*François Delarue,Pierre Lavigne*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出熵惩罚鲁棒平均场博弈问题，其中代表性智能体与Nature对抗，Nature以熵成本扭曲参考概率测度，建立均衡存在性并引入联合平坦反单调性和位移单调性条件保证唯一性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在不确定性环境下，当智能体面临对手（Nature）可能扭曲概率测度时的鲁棒平均场博弈问题，扩展经典平均场博弈框架以处理模型不确定性

Method: 将智能体目标建模为min-max随机控制问题，Nature以熵成本扭曲参考概率测度；使用Schauder不动点定理证明均衡存在性；引入联合平坦反单调性和位移单调性条件确保唯一性

Result: 建立了熵惩罚鲁棒平均场博弈均衡的存在性，在联合平坦反单调性和位移单调性条件下证明了均衡唯一性，并展示了两个N-玩家博弈类别的均值场极限产生ε-纳什均衡

Conclusion: 成功扩展了平均场博弈理论到鲁棒设置，建立了存在性和唯一性结果，为处理模型不确定性提供了理论框架，并验证了均值场极限的近似纳什均衡性质

Abstract: In this article, we introduce a new class of entropy-penalized robust mean field game problems in which the representative agent is opposed to Nature. The agent's objective is formulated as a min-max stochastic control problem, in which Nature distorts the reference probability measure at an entropic cost. As a consequence, the distribution of the continuum of agents represented by the player is given by the effective measure induced by Nature. Existence of a mean-field game equilibrium is established via a Schauder fixed point argument. To ensure uniqueness, we introduce a joint flat anti-monotonicity and displacement monotonicity condition, extending the classical Lasry-Lions monotonicity framework. Finally, we present two classes of N -player games for which the mean-field game limit yields $ε$-Nash equilibria.

</details>


### [188] [Robust Discrete Pricing Optimization via Multiple-Choice Knapsack Reductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18653)
*Zi Yuan Eric Shao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究离散投资组合定价问题，在利润率和公平约束下从有限菜单中为每个产品选择一个价格，采用预算化鲁棒公式处理需求不确定性，通过转化为MCKP问题并利用LP松弛的结构特性获得精确解法，证明整数间隙有界且相对间隙以O(1/n)衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究离散投资组合定价问题，需要在需求不确定性的情况下为每个产品从有限价格菜单中选择一个价格，同时满足利润率和公平约束。需要一种既能控制保守性又保持计算可行性的方法。

Method: 采用预算化鲁棒公式处理需求不确定性，将问题转化为多选择背包问题（MCKP）。识别LP松弛的结构特性，特别是上包络过滤和包络段上的贪婪填充，为固定参数子问题的LP松弛提供精确解法。

Result: 证明固定参数子问题的整数间隙有界于单项目包络跳跃的加法界，在标准有界性和线性增长假设下，相对间隙以O(1/n)衰减。数值实验表明，在测试实例上，鲁棒利润率保护可以实现小于1%的名义收入损失。

Conclusion: 提出的方法为离散投资组合定价问题提供了计算可行的鲁棒解决方案，能够有效处理需求不确定性，在保持利润率的同时最小化收入损失，理论分析和数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: We study a discrete portfolio pricing problem that selects one price per product from a finite menu under margin and fairness constraints. To account for demand uncertainty, we incorporate a budgeted robust formulation that controls conservatism while remaining computationally tractable. By reducing the problem to a Multiple-Choice Knapsack Problem (MCKP), we identify structural properties of the LP relaxation, in particular upper-hull filtering and greedy filling over hull segments, that yield an exact solution method for the LP relaxation of the fixed-parameter subproblems. For the resulting fixed-parameter subproblems, we show that the integrality gap is bounded additively by a single-item hull jump, and that the corresponding relative gap decays as O(1/n) under standard boundedness and linear-growth assumptions. Numerical experiments on synthetic portfolios and a stylized retail case study with economically calibrated parameters are consistent with these bounds and indicate that robust margin protection can be achieved with less than 1 percent nominal revenue loss on the instances tested.

</details>


### [189] [Stabilization of Integral Difference Equations by solving a Corona problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18703)
*Adam Braun,Jean Auriol,Lucas Brivadis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种针对具有点式和分布式输入延迟的积分差分方程系统的稳定状态反馈控制律，通过积分算子作用于状态和输入历史，基于Corona问题的卷积方程求解控制器核函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IDE（积分差分方程）稳定化方法存在局限性，需要扩展至更一般的设置，允许任意数量的点式延迟同时影响状态和输入，且不要求延迟具有可公度性假设。

Method: 提出通过积分算子表达的状态反馈控制律，控制器核函数作为Corona问题产生的卷积方程的解。在适当谱可稳定条件下确保解的存在性，并采用最小二乘程序进行数值求解。

Result: 建立了闭环稳定性，将现有IDE稳定化结果扩展到更一般的设置，允许任意数量的点式延迟同时影响状态和输入，无需可公度性假设。

Conclusion: 该方法成功扩展了IDE系统的稳定化理论，为具有混合延迟结构的控制系统提供了有效的稳定化框架，并通过数值方法实现了控制器核函数的求解。

Abstract: This paper proposes a stabilizing state-feedback control law for vector-valued state systems with a scalar control input, governed by a general class of integral difference equations that incorporate both pointwise and distributed input delays. The proposed controller is expressed through integral operators acting on the state and input histories over a finite time horizon. Closed-loop stability is established by characterizing the controller kernels as solutions to a convolution equation arising from a Corona problem. The existence of such solutions is ensured under a suitable spectral stabilizability condition, and a least-square procedure is implemented to find them numerically. The approach extends existing IDE stabilization results to more general settings, allowing for arbitrary numbers of pointwise delays affecting both the state and input, without requiring commensurability assumptions.

</details>


### [190] [A mathematical framework for time-delay reservoir computing analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18706)
*Anh-Tuan Clabaut,Jean Auriol,Islam Boussaada,Guilherme Mazanti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 为时延储备池计算建立控制理论框架，提供分离性和渐消记忆的严格数学定义，推导线性储备池的分离距离下界，并通过数值实验验证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储备池计算虽然实验进展显著，但其核心特性（分离性、鲁棒性、渐消记忆）仍缺乏严格的数学理论基础，本文旨在填补这一空白

Method: 引入基于泛函范数的分离性和渐消记忆的正式定义，建立与时延系统稳定性概念（增量输入到状态稳定性）的联系，对线性储备池通过傅里叶分析推导分离距离的显式下界

Result: 建立了时延储备池计算的控制理论分析框架，为线性储备池提供了可计算的分离距离下界，在NARMA10基准和连续时间系统预测任务上验证了方法的有效性

Conclusion: 本文为储备池计算提供了首个严格的数学基础，将控制理论概念与储备池特性联系起来，为储备池设计提供了理论指导，并通过最小化数字实现验证了框架的实用性

Abstract: Reservoir computing is a well-established approach for processing data with a much lower complexity compared to traditional neural networks. Despite two decades of experimental progress, the core properties of reservoir computing (namely separation, robustness, and fading memory) still lack rigorous mathematical foundations. This paper addresses this gap by providing a control-theoretic framework for the analysis of time-delay-based reservoir computers. We introduce formal definitions of the separation property and fading memory in terms of functional norms, and establish their connection to well-known stability notions for time-delay systems as incremental input-to-state stability. For a class of linear reservoirs, we derive an explicit lower bound for the separation distance via Fourier analysis, offering a computable criterion for reservoir design. Numerical results on the NARMA10 benchmark and continuous-time system prediction validate the approach with a minimal digital implementation.

</details>


### [191] [Non-linear in-band interference cancellation on base of conjugate gradients method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18727)
*Alexander Degtyarev,Sergei Bakhurin,Nikita Yudin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于混合牛顿法的共轭梯度方法，用于全双工通信系统中多层非线性自干扰消除器的自适应优化，在收敛速度和计算复杂度之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工通信系统中的数字域自干扰消除采用多层非线性模型，但传统二阶方法因Hessian矩阵不定性而受限，混合牛顿法计算成本过高，需要一种兼顾收敛速度和计算效率的优化方法。

Method: 提出基于混合牛顿法的共轭梯度方法，利用损失函数的混合二阶导数信息，避免显式计算完整的Hessian矩阵，在梯度下降框架中融入二阶信息。

Result: 该方法比一阶方法具有更高的收敛速度，同时比传统二阶方法显著降低计算资源需求，适用于多层非线性自干扰消除器的自适应优化。

Conclusion: 基于混合牛顿法的共轭梯度方法有效解决了全双工通信系统中自干扰消除器优化的收敛速度与计算复杂度之间的矛盾，为实际系统实现提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates one possible solution to the problem of self-interference cancellation (SIC) arising in the design of in-band full-duplex (IBFD) communication systems. Self-interference cancellation is performed in the digital domain using multilayer nonlinear models adapted via gradient-based optimization. The presence of local minima and saddle points during the adaptation of multilayer models limits the direct use of second-order methods due to the indefiniteness of the hessian matrix. The mixed Newton method can address the saddle-point issue; however, it requires significant computational resources. In this work, a conjugate gradient (CG) method constructed on the base of the mixed Newton method (MNM) is proposed. The method exploits information from mixed second-order derivatives of the loss function without explicit computation the full hessian matrix. As a result, the proposed approach achieves a higher convergence rate than first-order methods while requiring significantly lower computational resources than conventional second-order methods when adapting multilayer nonlinear self-interference cancellers in full-duplex communication systems.

</details>


### [192] [Scheduling Ground-Based Telescope Observations with Uncertain Nights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18730)
*Thomas Rahab Lacroix,Pierre Lemaire,Anne-Marie Lagrange,Julien Milli,Nadia Brauner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了一种基于随机优化的数学方法，用于在天气不确定条件下调度地基望远镜观测，以提高望远镜使用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地基望远镜观测受气象条件（云、风、大气湍流）影响，观测时间昂贵（ESO每夜8.3万欧元），需要优化调度以充分利用每一秒观测时间。

Method: 采用随机优化和反应式策略的数学方法，考虑多个观测目标、不确定的晴朗夜晚数量以及各种观测约束条件。

Result: 通过与其他方法比较，证明了基于随机优化和反应式策略的方法在不确定天气条件下的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 在天气不确定条件下，采用随机优化方法进行望远镜观测调度可以显著提高观测效率，自动化工具支持对优化望远镜使用至关重要。

Abstract: The observation of celestial objects is a fundamental activity in astronomy. Ground-based and space telescopes are used to gather electromagnetic radiation from space, allowing astronomers to study a wide range of celestial objects and phenomena, such as stars, planets, galaxies, and black holes. The European Southern Observatory (ESO) charges each night 83 kEUR (Milli et al. 2019), so the schedules of the telescopes are really important in order to optimize every second. Ground-based telescopes are affected by meteorological conditions, such as clouds, wind, and atmospheric turbulence. Accurate scheduling of observations in the presence of such uncertainties can significantly improve the efficiency of telescopes use and support from automated tools is highly desirable. In this paper, we study a mathematical approach for scheduling ground-based telescope observations under an uncertain number of clear nights due to uncertain weather and atmospheric conditions. The model considers multiple targets, uncertain number of nights, and various observing constraints. We demonstrate the viability and effectiveness of an approach based on stochastic optimization and reactive strategy comparing it against other methods.

</details>


### [193] [Second-Order Conditions for Infinite-Horizon Semilinear Parabolic Control Problems without Tikhonov Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18745)
*Eduardo Casas,Nicolai Jork*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究无Tikhonov正则化的无限时域半线性抛物最优控制问题，建立二阶充分最优性条件，证明有限时域局部最优解收敛于无限时域解


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无限时域半线性抛物最优控制问题，该问题包含Neumann边界条件、控制约束，且成本泛函中无Tikhonov正则化项。主要动机是将经典有限时域理论扩展到无限时域问题，解决无正则化带来的理论困难。

Method: 考虑半线性抛物最优控制问题，控制约束在时间上是逐点的，在空间上可以是逐点或积分的。采用无限时域框架，通过分析无Tikhonov正则化的特殊情况，建立二阶充分最优性条件，并研究有限时域问题解向无限时域问题解的收敛性。

Result: 建立了无限时域问题的二阶充分最优性条件，证明了当时间域趋于无穷时，有限时域问题的局部最优状态收敛于无限时域问题的局部最优状态。

Conclusion: 成功将有限时域最优控制理论扩展到无限时域问题，即使在没有Tikhonov正则化的情况下，也能建立充分最优性条件并证明收敛性，为无限时域半线性抛物最优控制问题提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We consider semilinear parabolic optimal control problems subject to Neumann boundary conditions, control constraints, and an infinite time horizon. The control constraints are pointwise in time, but they can be pointwise or integral in the space variable. Crucially, the optimal control problem does not include a Tikhonov regularization in the cost functional, which provides a major difficulty in the extension of the classical finite-horizon theory to infinite-horizon optimal control problems. As a consequence of our findings, we establish a sufficient second-order optimality condition and prove that local optimal states of the finite-horizon problems approximate local optimal states to the infinite-horizon problem as the horizon tends to infinity.

</details>


### [194] [A new optimal control algorithm for the Keller-Segel problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18889)
*F. Guillen-Gonzalez,F. Palmero-Ramos,M. A. Rodriguez-Bellido,G. Tierra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对Keller-Segel化学趋化系统的最优控制算法，结合边界控制和分布式控制，通过离散化状态问题并使用梯度下降方法求解


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发针对Keller-Segel化学趋化系统的最优控制方法，该系统在生物学和医学中具有重要应用，需要有效控制化学物质在系统中的引入和移除

Method: 采用空间上风有限体积格式和时间半隐式有限差分对状态问题进行离散化，通过离散伴随方案计算离散成本函数的梯度，使用Adam梯度下降方法最小化约简成本泛函

Result: 提出的算法能够有效求解Keller-Segel系统的最优控制问题，数值结果验证了方法的效率和可行性

Conclusion: 该方法为Keller-Segel化学趋化系统的控制问题提供了一种有效的数值解决方案，结合了离散化技术和优化算法

Abstract: In this work we introduce a new optimal control algorithm for the Keller-Segel chemo-attraction system, where both boundary and distributed controls are considered and both are associated with introducing/removing the amount of chemical substances in the system. The key idea of our approach is to design the optimal control algorithm after discretizing the state problem system, which is done using an upwind finite volume scheme in space and a semi-implicit finite difference in time. Then, the discrete optimal control is approximated identifying the gradient of the reduced discrete cost via the discrete adjoint scheme. Finally, to minimize the reduced cost functional, we use a gradient descent type method (Adam scheme). Moreover, several numerical results are presented to illustrate the efficiency of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [195] [Optimal strategies for controlled growth in metastable Kawasaki dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18903)
*Simone Baldassarri,Maike C. de Jongh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文为二维正方晶格上Kawasaki动力学下的低温亚稳态Ising模型建立了马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）框架，研究外部控制器如何通过适时添加和移动粒子引导系统达到全占据态，并分析了两种奖励结构下的最优策略差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在有限二维晶格中，如何通过外部控制引导低温亚稳态Ising系统从初始状态演化到全占据态，探索不同优化目标（时间效率vs能量成本）对控制策略的影响。

Method: 建立Kawasaki动力学下的MDP模型，构造约束于单簇配置的简化MDP（其中粒子附着概率高于脱离），分析两种奖励结构：仅依赖到达目标时间的效率型奖励，以及包含动作相关能量成本的能耗型奖励。

Result: 在MDP框架下精确刻画了两种奖励结构的最优策略，发现它们具有不同行为：纯效率标准促进从簇边界中心生长，而能量奖励函数则偏好从簇角落生长。

Conclusion: 不同优化目标导致Ising系统控制策略的根本差异，效率优先策略与能量成本最优策略在簇生长位置选择上呈现对立特征，为复杂系统的优化控制提供了理论洞察。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a Markov decision process (MDP) formulation for the low--temperature metastable Ising model evolving according to Kawasaki dynamics in a finite box of the two--dimensional square lattice. We analyze how an external controller can guide the system to the all--occupied state by appropriately adding and moving particles at specified moments in time. To this end, we construct a reduced MDP on a constrained family of configurations having a single cluster, a regime where particle attachment is more likely than detachment. We investigate two reward structures: one that depends solely on the time to reach the target configuration, and another that incorporates action--dependent energy costs. Within this MDP framework, we characterize the exact optimal policies under both reward structures, which turn out to have a different behavior: while a purely efficiency--based criterion promotes the growth from the boundary centers of the cluster, an energy--based reward function favours the growth at the corners of the cluster.

</details>


### [196] [A heavy-ball type curve search method for smooth convexly constrained optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19032)
*Federica Donnini,Pierluigi Mansueto*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种适用于凸约束优化的重球型方法，通过曲线搜索框架确保可行性，证明了算法的全局收敛性，并在数值实验中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然重球法等动量技术在无约束优化中能加速收敛，但在约束优化中应用有限，主要挑战在于保持可行性和确保收敛性。因此需要开发一种能在凸约束优化中有效应用动量技术的方法。

Method: 提出了一种重球型方法，结合曲线搜索全局化框架：算法尝试动量更新，当动量步不可行或不满足Armijo型下降条件时，通过曲线搜索平滑地回退到可行下降方向。还讨论了非单调曲线搜索、谱步长选择和自适应动量策略等增强机制。

Result: 证明了算法是良定义的且能全局收敛到稳定点。数值实验表明该方法在基准问题上具有鲁棒性，且与现有最优方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 成功将重球型动量技术扩展到凸约束优化问题，通过曲线搜索框架解决了可行性保持和收敛性证明的挑战，为约束优化中的加速方法提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper addresses smooth convexly constrained optimization problems where the Euclidean projection onto the feasible set is computationally tractable.
